{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_pP0W4Fsu0Lm0WZqOpwtOPEZj","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/app/services/sla_service.py","content":"from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom ..models import Priority, Ticket\n\nSLA_HOURS = {Priority.URGENT: 2, Priority.NORMAL: 24}\n\n\ndef utc_now() -> datetime:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef calculate_deadline(created_at: datetime, priority: str) -> datetime:\n    return created_at + timedelta(hours=SLA_HOURS[Priority(priority)])\n\n\ndef is_overdue(ticket: Ticket, now: datetime | None = None) -> bool:\n    if ticket.status == \"RESOLVED\":\n        return False\n    return (now or utc_now()) >= ticket.sla_deadline\n"}},"id":"6ae61235-1e2d-4e63-ad8a-273d27851066","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:47:54.508Z","parentId":"047cc4bf-6a67-4adf-944b-13f4899f1889"}
{"type":"tool.execution_complete","data":{"toolCallId":"call_pP0W4Fsu0Lm0WZqOpwtOPEZj","success":true},"id":"18d95a01-8d84-4a94-85e1-4b148ca7794d","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:47:54.795Z","parentId":"6ae61235-1e2d-4e63-ad8a-273d27851066"}
{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_4Bn0O6YusuiF0EGKWxxHJX64","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/app/services/queue_service.py","content":"from datetime import datetime\n\nfrom ..models import Ticket, TicketStatus\nfrom .sla_service import is_overdue\n\n\ndef queue_key(ticket: Ticket, now: datetime) -> tuple[int, int, float, float, int]:\n    \"\"\"Order active work by breach state, priority, deadline, age, then id.\"\"\"\n    overdue_rank = 0 if is_overdue(ticket, now) else 1\n    priority_rank = 0 if ticket.priority == \"URGENT\" else 1\n    return (\n        overdue_rank,\n        priority_rank,\n        ticket.sla_deadline.timestamp(),\n        ticket.created_at.timestamp(),\n        ticket.id,\n    )\n\n\ndef order_queue(tickets: list[Ticket], now: datetime) -> list[Ticket]:\n    active = [ticket for ticket in tickets if ticket.status != TicketStatus.RESOLVED]\n    return sorted(active, key=lambda ticket: queue_key(ticket, now))\n\n\ndef explain_ticket(ticket: Ticket, ordered: list[Ticket], now: datetime) -> list[str]:\n    reasons: list[str] = []\n    if is_overdue(ticket, now):\n        reasons.append(\"SLA breached\")\n    if ticket.priority == \"URGENT\":\n        reasons.append(\"URGENT priority\")\n    comparable = [other for other in ordered if other.priority == ticket.priority and is_overdue(other, now) == is_overdue(ticket, now)]\n    if comparable and ticket.sla_deadline == min(item.sla_deadline for item in comparable):\n        reasons.append(\"Closest deadline\")\n    if comparable and ticket.created_at == min(item.created_at for item in comparable if item.sla_deadline == ticket.sla_deadline):\n        reasons.append(\"Waiting longer than other comparable tickets\")\n    return reasons\n"}},"id":"b30cb441-2cf3-4f28-b192-f08dca7aadbb","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:47:54.800Z","parentId":"18d95a01-8d84-4a94-85e1-4b148ca7794d"}

csv\\nimport io\\nfrom datetime import datetime\\n\\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query\\nfrom fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse\\nfrom sqlalchemy import func, or_, select\\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session, joinedload\\n\\nfrom ..database import get_db\\nfrom ..models import Agent, Customer, Priority, Ticket, TicketActivity, TicketStatus\\nfrom ..schemas import ActivityOut, AgentOut, CustomerOut, TicketCreate, TicketOut, TicketUpdate\\nfrom ..services.queue_service import explain_ticket, order_queue\\nfrom ..services.sla_service import calculate_deadline, is_overdue, utc_now\\n\\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\\\"/api\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef ticket_out(ticket: Ticket, now: datetime | None = None) -> TicketOut:\\n    return TicketOut(\\n        id=ticket.id, ticket_number=ticket.ticket_number, title=ticket.title,\\n        description=ticket.description, priority=ticket.priority, status=ticket.status,\\n        customer_id=ticket.customer_id, customer_name=ticket.customer.name,\\n        assigned_to_id=ticket.assigned_to_id, assignee_name=ticket.assignee.name if ticket.assignee else None,\\n        created_at=ticket.created_at, updated_at=ticket.updated_at,\\n        sla_deadline=ticket.sla_deadline, resolved_at=ticket.resolved_at,\\n        overdue=is_overdue(ticket, now),\\n    )\\n\\n\\ndef load_tickets(db: Session, search: str | None, priority: str | None, status: str | None, assigned_to: int | None, unassigned: bool) -> list[Ticket]:\\n    query = select(Ticket).options(joinedload(Ticket.customer), joinedload(Ticket.assignee))\\n    if search:\\n        term = f\\\"%{search}%\\\"\\n        query = query.join(Customer).where(or_(Customer.name.ilike(term), Ticket.title.ilike(term), Ticket.ticket_number.ilike(term)))\\n    if priority:\\n        query = query.where(Ticket.priority == priority)\\n    if status:\\n        query = query.where(Ticket.status == status)\\n    if assigned_to is not None:\\n        query = query.where(Ticket.assigned_to_id == assigned_to)\\n    if unassigned:\\n        query = query.where(Ticket.assigned_to_id.is_(None))\\n    return list(db.scalars(query).unique().all())\\n\\n\\n@router.get(\\\"/health\\\")\\ndef health() -> dict[str, str]:\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\"}\\n\\n\\n@router.get(\\\"/agents\\\", response_model=list[AgentOut])\\ndef agents(db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[Agent]:\\n    return list(db.scalars(select(Agent).where(Agent.is_active.is_(True)).order_by(Agent.name)).all())\\n\\n\\n@router.get(\\\"/customers\\\", response_model=list[CustomerOut])\\ndef customers(q: str | None = None, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[Customer]:\\n    query = select(Customer).order_by(Customer.name)\\n    if q:\\n        query = query.where(Customer.name.ilike(f\\\"%{q}%\\\"))\\n    return list(db.scalars(query).all())\\n\\n\\n@router.get(\\\"/customers/{customer_id}\\\")\\ndef customer_detail(customer_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:\\n    customer = db.scalar(select(Customer).options(joinedload(Customer.tickets)).where(Customer.id == customer_id))\\n    if not customer:\\n        raise HTTPException(404, \\\"Customer not found\\\")\\n    now = utc_now()\\n    history = [ticket_out(ticket, now) for ticket in sorted(customer.tickets, key=lambda item: item.created_at, reverse=True)]\\n    return {\\\"customer\\\": CustomerOut.model_validate(customer), \\\"active_count\\\": sum(item.status != TicketStatus.RESOLVED for item in customer.tickets), \\\"resolved_count\\\": sum(item.status == TicketStatus.RESOLVED for item in customer.tickets), \\\"tickets\\\": history}\\n\\n\\n@router.get(\\\"/tickets\\\")\\ndef list_tickets(page: int = Query(1, ge=1), page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100), search: str | None = None, priority: str | None = None, status: str | None = None, assigned_to: int | None = None, unassigned: bool = False, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:\\n    now = utc_now()\\n    ordered = order_queue(load_tickets(db, search, priority, status, assigned_to, unassigned), now)\\n    start = (page - 1) * page_size\\n    return {\\\"items\\\": [ticket_out(ticket, now) for ticket in    assert result[0].ticket_number == \\\"breached\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef test_closer_deadline_wins_same_priority():\\n    result = order_queue([ticket(\\\"later\\\", \\\"URGENT\\\", 2), ticket(\\\"soon\\\", order_queue([ticket(\\\"resolved\\\", \\\"URGENT\\\", -4, status=\\\"RESOLVED\\\"), ticket(\\\"active\\\", \\\"NORMAL\\\", 2)], NOW)\\n    assert [item.ticket_number for item in result] == [\\\"active\\\"]\\n\\n\\ndef test_exact_deadline_is_overdue():\\n    result = order_queue([ticket(\\\"due\\\", \\\"NORMAL\\\", 0), ticket(\\\"later\\\", \\\"NORMAL\\\", 1)], NOW)\\n    assert result[0].ticket_number == \\\"due\\\"\\n\"}","type":"function"},{"toolCallId":"call_hYYWT4zxcJLw1QTFaQAGGobv","name":"create_file","arguments":"{\"filePath\":\"/workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/tests/__init__.py\",\"content\":\"\"}","type":"function"}]},"id":"f0268aa1-b1d5-42dc-97ad-f1e62707c8d5","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:52:06.829Z","parentId":"2e7373b7-ee56-4a33-885b-352b94cbb186"}

csv\nimport io\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query\nfrom fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse\nfrom sqlalchemy import func, or_, select\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session, joinedload\n\nfrom ..database import get_db\nfrom ..models import Agent, Customer, Priority, Ticket, TicketActivity, TicketStatus\nfrom ..schemas import ActivityOut, AgentOut, CustomerOut, TicketCreate, TicketOut, TicketUpdate\nfrom ..services.queue_service import explain_ticket, order_queue\nfrom ..services.sla_service import calculate_deadline, is_overdue, utc_now\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/api\")\n\n\ndef ticket_out(ticket: Ticket, now: datetime | None = None) -> TicketOut:\n    return TicketOut(\n        id=ticket.id, ticket_number=ticket.ticket_number, title=ticket.title,\n        description=ticket.description, priority=ticket.priority, status=ticket.status,\n        customer_id=ticket.customer_id, customer_name=ticket.customer.name,\n        assigned_to_id=ticket.assigned_to_id, assignee_name=ticket.assignee.name if ticket.assignee else None,\n        created_at=ticket.created_at, updated_at=ticket.updated_at,\n        sla_deadline=ticket.sla_deadline, resolved_at=ticket.resolved_at,\n        overdue=is_overdue(ticket, now),\n    )\n\n\ndef load_tickets(db: Session, search: str | None, priority: str | None, status: str | None, assigned_to: int | None, unassigned: bool) -> list[Ticket]:\n    query = select(Ticket).options(joinedload(Ticket.customer), joinedload(Ticket.assignee))\n    if search:\n        term = f\"%{search}%\"\n        query = query.join(Customer).where(or_(Customer.name.ilike(term), Ticket.title.ilike(term), Ticket.ticket_number.ilike(term)))\n    if priority:\n        query = query.where(Ticket.priority == priority)\n    if status:\n        query = query.where(Ticket.status == status)\n    if assigned_to is not None:\n        query = query.where(Ticket.assigned_to_id == assigned_to)\n    if unassigned:\n        query = query.where(Ticket.assigned_to_id.is_(None))\n    return list(db.scalars(query).unique().all())\n\n\n@router.get(\"/health\")\ndef health() -> dict[str, str]:\n    return {\"status\": \"ok\"}\n\n\n@router.get(\"/agents\", response_model=list[AgentOut])\ndef agents(db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[Agent]:\n    return list(db.scalars(select(Agent).where(Agent.is_active.is_(True)).order_by(Agent.name)).all())\n\n\n@router.get(\"/customers\", response_model=list[CustomerOut])\ndef customers(q: str | None = None, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[Customer]:\n    query = select(Customer).order_by(Customer.name)\n    if q:\n        query = query.where(Customer.name.ilike(f\"%{q}%\"))\n    return list(db.scalars(query).all())\n\n\n@router.get(\"/customers/{customer_id}\")\ndef customer_detail(customer_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:\n    customer = db.scalar(select(Customer).options(joinedload(Customer.tickets)).where(Customer.id == customer_id))\n    if not customer:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Customer not found\")\n    now = utc_now()\n    history = [ticket_out(ticket, now) for ticket in sorted(customer.tickets, key=lambda item: item.created_at, reverse=True)]\n    return {\"customer\": CustomerOut.model_validate(customer), \"active_count\": sum(item.status != TicketStatus.RESOLVED for item in customer.tickets), \"resolved_count\": sum(item.status == TicketStatus.RESOLVED for item in customer.tickets), \"tickets\": history}\n\n\n@router.get(\"/tickets\")\ndef list_tickets(page: int = Query(1, ge=1), page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100), search: str | None = None, priority: str | None = None, status: str | None = None, assigned_to: int | None = None, unassigned: bool = False, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:\n    now = utc_now()\n    ordered = order_queue(load_tickets(db, search, priority, status, assigned_to, unassigned), now)\n    start = (page - 1) * page_size\n    return {\"items\": [ticket_out(ticket, now) for ticket in ordered[start:start + page_size]], \"total\": len(ordered), \"page\": page, \"page_size\": page_size, \"explanation\": explain_ticket(ordered[0], ordered, now) if ordered else []}\n\n\n@router.get(\"/tickets/export\")\ndef export_tickets(search: str | None = None, priority: str | None = None, status: str | None = None, assigned_to: int | None = None, unassigned: bool = False, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> StreamingResponse:\n    now = utc_now()\n    output = io.StringIO()\n    writer = csv.writer(output)\n    writer.writerow([\"Ticket\", \"Customer\", \"Title\", \"Priority\", \"Status\", \"Assignee\", \"SLA deadline\", \"Overdue\"])\n    for ticket in order_queue(load_tickets(db, search, priority, status, assigned_to, unassigned), now):\n        writer.writerow([ticket.ticket_number, ticket.customer.name, ticket.title, ticket.priority, ticket.status, ticket.assignee.name if ticket.assignee else \"Unassigned\", ticket.sla_deadline.isoformat(), \"Yes\" if is_overdue(ticket, now) else \"No\"])\n    output.seek(0)\n    return StreamingResponse(iter([output.getvalue()]), media_type=\"text/csv\", headers={\"Content-Disposition\": \"attachment; filename=tickets.csv\"})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/tickets/{ticket_id}\", response_model=TicketOut)\ndef get_ticket(ticket_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> TicketOut:\n    ticket =   return ticket_out(ticket)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/tickets/{ticket_id}/activities\", response_model=list[ActivityOut])\ndef activities(ticket_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[TicketActivity]:\n    return list(db.scalars(select(TicketActivity).where(TicketActivity.ticket_id == ticket_id).order_by(TicketActivity.created_at.desc())).all())\n\n\ndef get_ticket_for_update(ticket_id: int, db: Session) -> Ticket:\n    ticket = db.scalar(select(Ticket).options(joinedload(Ticket.customer), joinedload(Ticket.assignee)).where(Ticket.id == ticket_id))\n    if not ticket:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Ticket not found\")\n    return ticket\n\n\ndef record(db: Session, ticket: Ticket, action: str, old: str | None, new: str | None) -> None:\n    db.add(TicketActivity(ticket_id=ticket.id, action=action, old_value=old, new_value=new, performed_by=\"Support team\", created_at=utc_now()))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/tickets\", response_model=TicketOut, status_code=201)\ndef create_ticket(payload: TicketCreate, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> TicketOut:\n    customer = db.get(Customer, payload.customer_id)\n    if not customer:\n        raise HTTPException(400, \"Customer not found\")\n    if payload.assigned_to_id is not None:\n        agent = db.get(Agent, payload.assigned_to_id)\n        if not agent or not agent.is_active:\n            raise HTTPException(400, \"Assignment target is not an active agent\")\n    now = utc_now()\n    ticket = Ticket(ticket_number=f\"HD-{now:%y%m%d%H%M%S}-{db.query(Ticket).count() + 1:03d}\", title=payload.title, description=payload.description, priority=payload.priority, status=TicketStatus.OPEN, customer_id=payload.customer_id, assigned_to_id=payload.assigned_to_id, created_at=now, updated_at=now, sla_deadline=calculate_deadline(now, payload.priority))\n    db.add(ticket)\n    db.flush()\n    record(db, ticket, \"CREATED\", None, ticket.ticket_number)\n    if payload.assigned_to_id:\n        record(db, ticket, \"ASSIGNED\", None, str(payload.assigned_to_id))\n    db.commit()\n    db.refresh(ticket)\n    return ticket_out(ticket, now)\n\n\n@router.patch(\"/tickets/{ticket_id}\", response_model=TicketOut)\ndef update_ticket(ticket_id: int, payload: TicketUpdate, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> TicketOut:\n    ticket = get_ticket_for_update(ticket_id, db)\n    changes = payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)\n    now = utc_now()\n    for field, value in changes.items():\n        if field == \"assigned_to_id\":\n            if value is not None and (not (agent := db.get(Agent, value)) or not agent.is_active):\n                raise HTTPException(400, \"Assignment target is not an active agent\")\n        old = getattr(ticket, field)\n        if old != value:\n            action = \"REASSIGNED\" if field == \"assigned_to_id\" else f\"{field.upper()}_CHANGED\"\n            record(db, ticket, action, str(old) if old is not None else None, str(value) if value is not None else None)\n            setattr(ticket, field, value)\n            if field == \"status\" and value == TicketStatus.RESOLVED:\n {"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_Ii2eIxnvNZXwFVj1JJrdrq2U","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/seed.py","content":"from datetime import timedelta\n\nfrom app.database import Base, SessionLocal, engine\nfrom app.models import Agent, Customer, Priority, Ticket, TicketActivity, TicketStatus\nfrom app.services.sla_service import calculate_deadline, utc_now\n\nBase.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)\ndb = SessionLocal()\nif not db.query(Customer).count():\n    now = utc_now()\n    customers = [Customer(name=name, email=f\"{name.lower().replace(' ', '.')}@example.com\", department=department, created_at=now) for name, department in [(\"Rahul Mehta\", \"Finance\"), (\"Aisha Khan\", \"Operations\"), (\"Marcus Lee\", \"Sales\"), (\"Sofia Garcia\", \"People\"), (\"Noah Williams\", \"Engineering\"), (\"Maya Patel\", \"Finance\"), (\"Ethan Brown\", \"Marketing\"), (\"Olivia Chen\", \"Operations\"), (\"Liam Smith\", \"Sales\"), (\"Zoe Martin\", \"Engineering\"), (\"Daniel Kim\", \"Support\")]]\n    agents = [Agent(name=name, email=f\"{name.lower().replace(' ', '.')}@helpdesk.example.com\", team=team, created_at=now) for name, team in [(\"Alex Morgan\", \"Platform\"), (\"Jordan Bell\", \"Workplace\"), (\"Sam Rivera\", \"Security\"), (\"Taylor Brooks\", \"Platform\"), (\"Casey Nguyen\", \"Workplace\"), (\"Morgan Ellis\", \"Security\")]]\n    db.add_all(customers + agents)\n    db.flush()\n    patterns = [(\"VPN access fails after laptop update\", \"Cannot connect to the corporate VPN from home.\", Priority.URGENT, TicketStatus.OPEN, 3, 0, -5), (\"Quarterly report permissions\", \"Please restore access to the reporting folder.\", Priority.NORMAL, TicketStatus.OPEN, 0, 1, -30), (\"New starter laptop setup\", \"Prepare a laptop and accounts for Monday.\", Priority.URGENT, TicketStatus.IN_PROGRESS, 1, 2, 1), (\"Printer queue stuck\", \"The second-floor printer is not processing jobs.\", Priority.NORMAL, TicketStatus.WAITING, 2, None, 4), (\"Expense portal question\", \"How do I update a cost center?\", Priority.NORMAL, TicketStatus.RESOLVED, 5, 3, -48)]\n    for index in range(55):\n        title, description, priority, status, customer_index, agent_index, age_hours = patterns[index % len(patterns)]\n        created = now - timedelta(hours=abs(age_hours) + (index % 7) * 2)\n        ticket = Ticket(ticket_number=f\"HD-{now:%y%m%d}-{index + 1:03d}\", title=title if index < 5 else f\"{title} #{index + 1}\", description=description, priority=priority, status=status, customer_id=customers[customer_index].id, assigned_to_id=agents[agent_index].id if agent_index is not None else None, created_at=created, updated_at=now, sla_deadline=calculate_deadline(created, priority), resolved_at=created + timedelta(hours=3) if status == TicketStatus.RESOLVED else None)\n        db.add(ticket)\n        db.flush()\n        db.add(TicketActivity(ticket_id=ticket.id, action=\"CREATED\", new_value=ticket.ticket_number, performed_by=\"System\", created_at=created))\n    db.commit()\ndb.close()\nprint(\"Seed complete\")\n"}},"id":"6be62078-8312-4c29-b14b-bde88c39a5a2","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:52:08.095Z","parentId":"cc9fb826-e4ce-4be0-8d19-01a27c549836"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_oisiSevNrdCyBKsiufmyspCS","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/tests/test_queue_service.py","content":"from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom app.models import Ticket\nfrom app.services.queue_service import order_queue\n\nNOW = datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef ticket(number, priority, deadline_offset, age_offset=0, status=\"OPEN\", ticket_id=1):\n    created = NOW - timedelta(hours=age_offset)\n    return Ticket(id=ticket_id, ticket_number=number, priority=priority, status=status, created_at=created, sla_deadline=NOW + timedelta(hours=deadline_offset))\n\n\ndef test_overdue_normal_beats_non_overdue_urgent():\n    result = order_queue([ticket(\"urgent\", \"URGENT\", 1), ticket(\"normal\", \"NORMAL\", -1)], NOW)\n    assert [item.ticket_number for item in result] == [\"normal\", \"urgent\"]\n\n\ndef test_overdue_urgent_beats_non_overdue_urgent():\n    result = order_queue([ticket(\"later\", \"URGENT\", 1), ticket(\"breached\", \"URGENT\", -1)], NOW)\n    assert result[0].ticket_number == \"breached\"\n\n\ndef test_closer_deadline_wins_same_priority():\n    result = order_queue([ticket(\"later\", \"URGENT\", 2), ticket(\"soon\", \"URGENT\", 1)], NOW)\n    assert result[0].ticket_number == \"soon\"\n\n\ndef test_older_wins_tied_deadline():\n    result = order_queue([ticket(\"new\", \"NORMAL\", 2, age_offset=1), ticket(\"old\", \"NORMAL\", 2, age_offset=4)], NOW)\n    assert result[0].ticket_number == \"old\"\n\n\ndef test_resolved_is_excluded():\n    result = order_queue([ticket(\"resolved\", \"URGENT\", -4, status=\"RESOLVED\"), ticket(\"active\", \"NORMAL\", 2)], NOW)\n    assert [item.ticket_number for item in result] == [\"active\"]\n\n\ndef test_exact_deadline_is_overdue():\n    result = order_queue([ticket(\"due\", \"NORMAL\", 0), ticket(\"later\", \"NORMAL\", 1)], NOW)\n    assert result[0].ticket_number == \"due\"\n"}},"id":"7817a937-8a75-4d2c-b7d6-94396a355d45","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:52:08.410Z","parentId":"7516d725-e857-4d3d-94bb-074458702260"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_T7RZRGKKo12u8oJ6DfnZX8UZ","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/frontend/src/App.jsx","content":"import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'\nimport { Link, Route, Routes, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom'\nimport { AlertCircle, ArrowUpRight, CheckCircle2, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Clock3, Download, Inbox, LayoutDashboard, Plus, Search, Users, X } from 'lucide-react'\n\nconst API = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:8000/api'\nconst filters = [['', 'All tickets'], ['OPEN', 'Open'], ['IN_PROGRESS', 'In progress'], ['WAITING', 'Waiting'], ['RESOLVED', 'Resolved']]\n\nfunction useApi(path, options) {\n  const [data, setData] = useState(null); const [error, setError] = useState(''); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)\n  useEffect(() => { let active = true; setLoading(true); fetch(`${API}${path}`, options).then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Unable to load helpdesk data'); return r.json() }).then(value => active && setData(value)).catch(e => active && setError(e.message)).finally(() => active && setLoading(false)); return () => { active = false } }, [path])\n  return { data, error, loading }\n}\n\nfunction formatSla(deadline, overdue) { const delta = new Date(deadline).getTime() - Date.now(); const minutes = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.abs(delta) / 60000)); const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60); const rest = minutes % 60; const value = hours ? `${hours}h ${rest}m` : `${rest}m`; return overdue ? `Overdue by ${value}` : `Due in ${value}` }\nfunction Header() { return <header className=\"topbar\"><Link className=\"brand\" to=\"/\"><span className=\"brand-mark\">S</span><span>smart<span className=\"brand-accent\">desk</span></span></Link><nav><Link to=\"/\">Queue</Link><Link to=\"/customers\">Customers</Link></nav><div className=\"header-user\"><span className=\"status-dot\"/> <span>Support team</span><span className=\"avatar\">ST</span></div></header> }\nfunction Stat({ icon: Icon, label, value, tone }) { return <div className={`stat stat-${tone}`}><div className=\"stat-icon\"><Icon size={18}/></div><div><p>{label}</p><strong>{value ?? '—'}</strong></div></div> }\nfunction Stats({ stats }) { return <section className=\"stats-grid\"><Stat icon={Inbox} label=\"Total tickets\" value={stats?.total} tone=\"ink\"/><Stat icon={AlertCircle} label=\"Overdue now\" value={stats?.overdue} tone=\"red\"/><Stat icon={ArrowUpRight} label=\"Urgent active\" value={stats?.urgent} tone=\"amber\"/><Stat icon={Users} label=\"Unassigned\" value={stats?.unassigned} tone=\"blue\"/><Stat icon={Clock3} label=\"Open\" value={stats?.open} tone=\"mint\"/><Stat icon={CheckCircle2} label=\"Resolved\" value={stats?.resolved} tone=\"gray\"/></section> }\nfunction Badge({ children, type }) { return <span className={`badge badge-${type?.toLowerCase().replace('_', '-')}`}>{children?.replace('_', ' ')}</span> }\nfunction TicketRow({ ticket, top }) { const navigate = useNavigate(); return <button className={`ticket-row ${top ? 'ticket-top' : ''}`} onClick={() => navigate(`/tickets/${ticket.id}`)}><div className=\"ticket-main\"><div className=\"ticket-number\">{ticket.ticket_number} {top && <span className=\"next-label\">NEXT UP</span>}</div><h3>{ticket.title}</h3><p>{ticket.customer_name} <span>·</span> {ticket.assignee_name || 'Unassigned'}</p></div><div className=\"ticket-meta\"><Badge type={ticket.priority}>{ticket.priority}</Badge><Badge type={ticket.status}>{ticket.status}</Badge><span className={`sla ${ticket.overdue ? 'sla-overdue' : ''}`}><Clock3 size={14}/>{formatSla(ticket.sla_deadline, ticket.overdue)}</span></div><ArrowUpRight className=\"row-arrow\" size={18}/></button> }\nfunction CreateTicket({ onClose }) { const [form, setForm] = useState({ title: '', description: '', customer_id: 1, priority: 'NORMAL' }); const [message, setMessage] = useState(''); const submit = async e => { e.preventDefault(); const response = await fetch(`${API}/tickets`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(form) }); if (response.ok) { setMessage('Ticket created and added to the queue.'); setTimeout(onClose, 700) } else setMessage('Please check the required fields.') }; return <div className=\"modal-backdrop\"><form className=\"modal\" onSubmit={submit}><button type=\"button\" className=\"icon-button close\" onClick={onClose}><X size={18}/></button><span className=\"eyebrow\">NEW REQUEST</span><h2>Create a ticket</h2><p className=\"modal-subtitle\">It will be placed automatically using SLA urgency and priority.</p><label>Title<input required value={form.title} onChange={e => setForm({...form, title: e.target.value})} placeholder=\"What needs attention?\"/></label><label>Description<textarea required value={form.description} onChange={e => setForm({...form, description: e.target.value})} placeholder=\"Add useful context for the team...\"/></label><label>Priority<select value={form.priority} onChange={e => setForm({...form, priority: e.target.value})}><option>NORMAL</option><option>URGENT</option></select></label>{message && <p className=\"form-message\">{message}</p>}<button className=\"primary-button\" type=\"submit\"><Plus size={17}/> Create ticket</button></form></div> }\nfunction Queue() { const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams(); const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false); const [tick, setTick] = useState(0); const page = Number(params.get('page') || 1); const search = params.get('search') || ''; const status = params.get('status') || ''; const priority = params.get('priority') || ''; const query = `/tickets?page=${page}&page_size=8&search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}&status=${status}&priority=${priority}`; const queue = useApi(query); const stats = useApi('/dashboard/stats'); useEffect(() => { const id = setInterval(() => setTick(value => value + 1), 30000); return () => clearInterval(id) }, []); const update = (key, value) => { const next = new URLSearchParams(params); value ? next.set(key, value) : next.delete(key); next.delete('page'); setParams(next) }; return <><Header/><main className=\"shell\"><div className=\"page-heading\"><div><span className=\"eyebrow\">OPERATIONS / LIVE QUEUE</span><h1>Good morning, keep work moving.</h1><p className=\"lede\">The next ticket is always determined by the SLA clock.</p></div><button className=\"primary-button\" onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)}><Plus size={17}/> New ticket</button></div><Stats stats={stats.data}/><section className=\"queue-section\"><div className=\"queue-heading\"><div><span className=\"eyebrow\">HANDLING ORDER</span><h2>Operational queue</h2></div>{queue.data && <span className=\"count-label\">{queue.data.total} active tickets</span>}</div><div className=\"controls\"><div className=\"search-control\"><Search size={17}/><input value={search} onChange={e => update('search', e.target.value)} placeholder=\"Search ticket, title, or customer...\"/></div><select value={priority} onChange={e => update('priority', e.target.value)}><option value=\"\">All priorities</option><option value=\"URGENT\">Urgent only</option><option value=\"NORMAL\">Normal only</option></select><div className=\"filter-pills\">{filters.map(([value, label]) => <button key={value} className={status === value ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => update('status', value)}>{label}</button>)}</div><a className=\"export-link\" href={`${API}/tickets/export`}><Download size={15}/> Export</a></div>{queue.loading ? <div className=\"empty-state\">Loading the live queue...</div> : queue.error ? <div className=\"empty-state error-state\">{queue.error}. Start the API at port 8000.</div> : <>{queue.data?.items?.length ? <div className=\"ticket-list\">{queue.data.items.map((ticket, index) => <TicketRow key={ticket.id} ticket={ticket} top={index === 0 && page === 1}/>)}</div> : <div className=\"empty-state\">No tickets match these filters.</div>}<div className=\"queue-footer\"><span>Showing {queue.data?.items.length ? ((page - 1) * 8) + 1 : 0}–{Math.min(page * 8, queue.data?.total || 0)} of {queue.data?.total || 0}</span><div><button className=\"page-button\" disabled={page === 1} onClick={() => update('page', String(page - 1))}><ChevronLeft size={16}/></button><span className=\"page-number\">{page}</span><button className=\"page-button\" disabled={page * 8 >= (queue.data?.total || 0)} onClick={() => update('page', String(page + 1))}><ChevronRight size={16}/></button></div></div></>}</section></main>{showCreate && <CreateTicket onClose={() => setShowCreate(false)}/>}</> }\nfunction TicketDetail() { const [params] = useSearchParams(); const path = window.location.pathname; const ticketId = path.split('/').pop(); const ticket = useApi(`/tickets/${ticketId}`); const activity = useApi(`/tickets/${ticketId}/activities`); if (ticket.loading) return <><Header/><main className=\"shell\"><div className=\"empty-state\">Loading ticket...</div></main></>; if (ticket.error) return <><Header/><main className=\"shell\"><div className=\"empty-state error-state\">{ticket.error}</div></main></>; const item = ticket.data; return <><Header/><main className=\"shell detail-shell\"><Link to=\"/\" className=\"back-link\"><ChevronLeft size={16}/> Back to queue</Link><div className=\"detail-header\"><div><span className=\"eyebrow\">{item.ticket_number}</span><h1>{item.title}</h1><p className=\"lede\">{item.customer_name} · opened {new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString()}</p></div><div className={`detail-sla ${item.overdue ? 'is-overdue' : ''}`}><Clock3 size={20}/><span>{formatSla(item.sla_deadline, item.overdue)}</span><small>SLA deadline {new Date(item.sla_deadline).toLocaleString()}</small></div></div><div className=\"detail-grid\"><article className=\"detail-card\"><div className=\"detail-badges\"><Badge type={item.priority}>{item.priority}</Badge><Badge type={item.status}>{item.status}</Badge></div><p className=\"description\">{item.description}</p><h3>Ticket activity</h3><div className=\"timeline\">{activity.data?.map(event => <div className=\"timeline-item\" key={event.id}><span className=\"timeline-dot\"/><div><strong>{event.action.replace('_', ' ')}</strong><p>{event.new_value || 'Recorded'} · {event.performed_by}</p><time>{new Date(event.created_at).toLocaleString()}</time></div></div>)}</div></article><aside className=\"detail-card facts\"><h3>Details</h3><dl><dt>Status</dt><dd>{item.status.replace('_', ' ')}</dd><dt>Assigned to</dt><dd>{item.assignee_name || 'Unassigned'}</dd><dt>Customer</dt><dd>{item.customer_name}</dd><dt>Last updated</dt><dd>{new Date(item.updated_at).toLocaleString()}</dd></dl></aside></div></main></> }\nfunction Customers() { const [search, setSearch] = useState(''); const customers = useApi(`/customers?q=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`); return <><Header/><main className=\"shell\"><div className=\"page-heading\"><div><span className=\"eyebrow\">DIRECTORY</span><h1>Customers</h1><p className=\"lede\">Search customer history and active work.</p></div></div><div className=\"search-control wide-search\"><Search size={17}/><input value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder=\"Search by customer name...\"/></div><div className=\"customer-grid\">{customers.data?.map(customer => <div className=\"customer-card\" key={customer.id}><div className=\"customer-avatar\">{customer.name.split(' ').map(x => x[0]).join('')}</div><div><h3>{customer.name}</h3><p>{customer.email}</p><span>{customer.department}</span></div></div>)}</div></main></> }\nexport default function App() { return <Routes><Route path=\"/\" element={<Queue/>}/><Route path=\"/tickets/:id\" element={<TicketDetail/>}/><Route path=\"/customers\" element={<Customers/>}/></Routes> }\n"}},"id":"66dfd815-b050-4c0d-8b02-d565bb433af1","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:54:13.076Z","parentId":"5892bb8c-eb61-4d2b-b866-f5dc7944bbc3"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"f4b86f65-ae69-4658-9518-1504e313d1cd","content":"The API smoke test found the important production edge case the prompt called out: SQLite returns stored timestamps without timezone metadata, while the service compares them to aware UTC time. I’m fixing the SLA helper at the boundary by normalizing database timestamps to UTC, then I’ll rerun the API smoke test and full backend suite.","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_nAdBsjOffIut420aNb6v4WJw","name":"apply_patch","arguments":"{\"input\":\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/app/services/sla_service.py\\n@@\\n def utc_now() -> datetime:\\n     return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\\n \\n \\n+def as_utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:\\n+    return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) if value.tzinfo is None else value.astimezone(timezone.utc)\\n+\\n+\\n def calculate_deadline(created_at: datetime, priority: str) -> datetime:\\n     return created_at + timedelta(hours=SLA_HOURS[Priority(priority)])\\n@@\\n def is_overdue(ticket: Ticket, now: datetime | None = None) -> bool:\\n     if ticket.status == \\\"RESOLVED\\\":\\n         return False\\n-    return (now or utc_now()) >= ticket.sla_deadline\\n+    return (now or utc_now()) >= as_utc(ticket.sla_deadline)\\n*** End Patch\",\"explanation\":\"Normalize naive database timestamps to UTC before overdue comparisons.\"}","type":"function"}],"reasoningText":""},"id":"548a740d-2756-4843-9176-bee21c8a68c2","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:55:24.655Z","parentId":"76a299d0-a3f6-4223-aa52-9e45a9545f07"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_nAdBsjOffIut420aNb6v4WJw","toolName":"apply_patch","arguments":{"input":"*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/app/services/sla_service.py\n@@\n def utc_now() -> datetime:\n     return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n \n \n+def as_utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:\n+    return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) if value.tzinfo is None else value.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n+\n+\n def calculate_deadline(created_at: datetime, priority: str) -> datetime:\n     return created_at + timedelta(hours=SLA_HOURS[Priority(priority)])\n@@\n def is_overdue(ticket: Ticket, now: datetime | None = None) -> bool:\n     if ticket.status == \"RESOLVED\":\n         return False\n-    return (now or utc_now()) >= ticket.sla_deadline\n+    return (now or utc_now()) >= as_utc(ticket.sla_deadline)\n*** End Patch","explanation":"Normalize naive database timestamps to UTC before overdue comparisons."}},"id":"3fe1db0c-8339-418b-86e4-6602e27024d8","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:55:24.902Z","parentId":"ea37895d-b79c-4ef1-b6af-bc6639930da7"}

op.drop_table(\"agents\")\n    op.drop_table(\"customers\")\n"}},"id":"f43b0fc5-0b8a-486a-b8e1-d54cfb9189f0","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:56:00.367Z","parentId":"1297e7f3-bcce-411c-b908-37d6c65b07f2"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"4f844049-d1b7-417d-9aaa-f14e3853170e","content":"The repository now has a Postgres Compose service, environment examples, migration scaffolding, and the explicit mutation routes required by the API spec. I’m documenting the exact run sequence and queue contract next, then I’ll run migration/build/test checks together and inspect the final worktree for accidental artifacts.","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_Dj2qJCgz0bCMw7J0I1ZTSU2T","name":"apply_patch","arguments":"{\"input\":\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/requirements.txt\\n@@\\n sqlalchemy==2.0.45\\n+alembic==1.14.1\\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/README.md\\n@@\\n-# Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment\\n+# Smartdesk\\n+\\n+SLA-aware IT helpdesk queue for teams that need the next best ticket to be obvious. The queue is calculated by the FastAPI backend, not by the browser: overdue active work always leads, then urgent priority, nearest deadline, oldest creation time, and ticket ID as a deterministic tie-breaker. Resolved tickets never compete with operational work.\\n+\\n+## What is included\\n+\\n+- FastAPI + SQLAlchemy 2.x domain API with PostgreSQL configuration and SQLite fallback for quick local testing.\\n+- Dedicated SLA and queue services with timezone-aware deadline handling and generated queue explanations.\\n+- React/Vite/Tailwind dashboard with live countdown labels, search, status/priority filters, pagination, CSV export, customer directory, ticket detail activity, and create-ticket flow.\\n+- Normalized customers, agents, tickets, and ticket activities with useful indexes.\\n+- Alembic initial migration, realistic seed data (11 customers, 6 agents, 55 tickets), Docker Compose PostgreSQL, and pytest queue coverage.\\n+\\n+## Architecture\\n+\\n+```mermaid\\n+flowchart LR\\n+  Browser[React + Vite] -->|REST| API[FastAPI routers]\\n+  API --> Queue[Queue service]\\n+  API --> SLA[SLA service]\\n+  API --> ORM[SQLAlchemy]\\n+  ORM --> DB[(PostgreSQL)]\\n+  ORM --> SQLite[(SQLite test fallback)]\\n+```\\n+\\n+## Run locally\\n+\\n+Requirements: Python 3.11+ and Node 20+. Python 3.14 is supported by the current dependency pins.\\n+\\n+```bash\\n+cp .env.example .env\\n+docker compose up -d postgres\\n+python -m venv .venv && source .venv/bin/activate\\n+pip install -r backend/requirements.txt\\n+alembic -c backend/alembic.ini upgrade head\\n+PYTHONPATH=backend python backend/seed.py\\n+PYTHONPATH=backend uvicorn app.main:app --reload --port 8000\\n+```\\n+\\n+In a second terminal:\\n+\\n+```bash\\n+npm install --prefix frontend\\n+npm run dev --prefix frontend\\n+```\\n+\\n+Open `http://localhost:5173`. For a zero-setup demo, omit Docker and `.env`; the backend defaults to `sqlite:///./helpdesk.db`.\\n+\\n+## Codespaces\\n+\\n+Open the repository in Codespaces, run the local commands above, and forward ports 5173 and 8000. The included `docker-compose.yml` provides only the required PostgreSQL service; optional infrastructure is intentionally absent until core queue behavior is proven.\\n+\\n+## API overview\\n+\\n+| Method | Endpoint | Purpose |\\n+| --- | --- | --- |\\n+| GET | `/api/tickets` | Filter, queue-order, and paginate tickets |\\n+| GET | `/api/tickets/export` | Export the same filtered ordered set as CSV |\\n+| GET | `/api/tickets/{id}` | Ticket details |\\n+| POST | `/api/tickets` | Create a ticket and initial activity |\\n+| PATCH | `/api/tickets/{id}` | Update ticket fields and log changes |\\n+| PATCH | `/api/tickets/{id}/assign` | Assign or unassign |\\n+| PATCH | `/api/tickets/{id}/status` | Change status |\\n+| PATCH | `/api/tickets/{id}/priority` | Change priority |\\n+| GET | `/api/tickets/{id}/activities` | Activity history |\\n+| GET | `/api/customers?q=` | Customer search |\\n+| GET | `/api/customers/{id}` | Customer history and counts |\\n+| GET | `/api/agents` | Active agents |\\n+| GET | `/api/dashboard/stats` | Dashboard counts |\\n+\\n+All list endpoints apply `FILTER -> QUEUE ORDER -> PAGINATION`. SLA deadlines are persisted once at creation using `URGENT = 2h` and `NORMAL = 24h`; overdue state is evaluated against the persisted deadline at request time.\\n+\\n+## Tests\\n+\\n+```bash\\n+PYTHONPATH=backend python -m pytest backend/tests -q\\n+npm run build --prefix frontend\\n+```\\n+\\n+The queue tests cover overdue precedence, priority, deadline and age tie-breakers, exact deadline behavior, and resolved exclusion. The seeded demo intentionally shows overdue normal tickets ahead of non-overdue urgent tickets.\\n+\\n+## Environment\\n+\\n+See `.env.example`: `DATABASE_URL`, `CORS_ORIGINS`, and `VITE_API_URL`. Never commit a real `.env` or database credentials.\\n+\\n+## Future improvements\\n+\\n+Authentication and role permissions, WebSocket queue updates, Redis caching, background SLA notifications, richer bulk actions, and a full API integration test suite can be added after the core workflow.\\n*** End Patch\",\"explanation\":\"Document the architecture, queue contract, setup, API, tests, and future extension path; add Alembic to backend dependencies.\"}","type":"function"}],"reasoningText":""},"id":"7cdb4076-4749-4ba0-8ff5-c8d6a39aef21","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:56:13.151Z","parentId":"526905c2-7470-4e8b-9b3f-76f12da7f254"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"5f5a9af8-5eb7-4666-82a0-ec4b2329156e","content":"Built a complete Smartdesk helpdesk application.\n\nImplemented:\n- FastAPI + SQLAlchemy backend with SLA-aware queue engine.\n- Queue ordering: overdue → urgent → nearest deadline → oldest ticket.\n- Ticket, customer, agent, activity, assignment, status, priority, search, filters, pagination, CSV export.\n- React/Vite/Tailwind dashboard with live SLA countdowns, queue explanation, ticket details, customer directory, and create-ticket flow.\n- PostgreSQL Docker Compose setup with SQLite fallback.\n- Alembic migration and realistic seed data: 11 customers, 6 agents, 55 tickets.\n- Queue tests covering overdue, priority, deadline, tie-breaking, resolved exclusion, and exact-deadline behavior.\n- Setup and architecture documentation in [README.md](README.md).\n\nValidation passed:\n- Alembic migration\n- Seed script\n- `6 passed` backend tests\n- Frontend production build\n- Static diagnostics: no errors\n\nRun with:\n\n```bash\ndocker compose up -d postgres\npip install -r backend/requirements.txt\nalembic -c backend/alembic.ini upgrade head\nPYTHONPATH=backend python backend/seed.py\nPYTHONPATH=backend uvicorn app.main:app --reload --port 8000\nnpm install --prefix frontend\nnpm run dev --prefix frontend\n```\n\nThe Vite build emits only a non-blocking module-format warning.","toolRequests":[],"reasoningText":""},"id":"f0333db6-3668-441c-bf94-3d2e397e4eac","timestamp":"2026-09-16T09:57:13.602Z","parentId":"f50bb272-0354-479d-b714-da38ab94c161"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_5XV56WQ2rAaVoO7XQlShkvVQ","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/backend/tests/test_escalation_service.py","content":"from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import create_engine, select\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n\nfrom app.database import Base\nfrom app.models import Customer, Ticket, TicketActivity, TicketStatus\nfrom app.services.escalation_service import escalate_breached_tickets\n\nNOW = datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef make_session() -> Session:\n    engine = create_engine(\"sqlite:///:memory:\")\n    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)\n    session = Session(engine)\n    customer = Customer(name=\"Test Customer\", email=\"test@example.com\", created_at=NOW)\n    session.add(customer)\n    session.flush()\n    session.add_all([\n        Ticket(ticket_number=\"HD-1\", title=\"Normal\", description=\"Needs help\", priority=\"NORMAL\", status=\"OPEN\", customer_id=customer.id, created_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=25), updated_at=NOW, sla_deadline=NOW - timedelta(hours=1)),\n        Ticket(ticket_number=\"HD-2\", title=\"Urgent\", description=\"Needs help\", priority=\"URGENT\", status=\"OPEN\", customer_id=customer.id, created_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=3), updated_at=NOW, sla_deadline=NOW - timedelta(hours=1)),\n        Ticket(ticket_number=\"HD-3\", title=\"Resolved\", description=\"Done\", priority=\"NORMAL\", status=TicketStatus.RESOLVED, customer_id=customer.id, created_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=25), updated_at=NOW, sla_deadline=NOW - timedelta(hours=1)),\n    ])\n    session.commit()\n    return session\n\n\ndef test_breached_normal_escalates_one_level_and_logs_activity():\n    session = make_session()\n    ticket = session.scalar(select(Ticket).where(Ticket.ticket_number == \"HD-1\"))\n    deadline = ticket.sla_deadline\n\n    escalated = escalate_breached_tickets(session, NOW)\n\n    session.refresh(ticket)\n    assert [item.ticket_number for item in escalated] == [\"HD-1\"]\n    assert ticket.priority == \"HIGH\"\n    assert ticket.sla_deadline == deadline\n    activity = session.scalar(select(TicketActivity).where(TicketActivity.ticket_id == ticket.id))\n    assert (activity.action, activity.old_value, activity.new_value) == (\"SLA_ESCALATED\", \"NORMAL\", \"HIGH\")\n\n\ndef test_second_run_advances_high_to_urgent_and_urgent_stays_urgent():\n    session = make_session()\n    escalate_breached_tickets(session, NOW)\n    escalated = escalate_breached_tickets(session, NOW)\n\n    assert [item.ticket_number for item in escalated] == [\"HD-1\"]\n    assert session.scalar(select(Ticket).where(Ticket.ticket_number == \"HD-1\")).priority == \"URGENT\"\n    assert session.scalar(select(Ticket).where(Ticket.ticket_number == \"HD-2\")).priority == \"URGENT\"\n\n\ndef test_resolved_ticket_is_never_escalated():\n    session = make_session()\n    escalate_breached_tickets(session, NOW)\n\n    ticket = session.scalar(select(Ticket).where(Ticket.ticket_number == \"HD-3\"))\n    assert ticket.priority == \"NORMAL\"\n    assert session.scalar(select(TicketActivity).where(TicketActivity.ticket_id == ticket.id)) is None\n"}},"id":"770b749a-1322-4e4b-b60a-2cb87041362e","timestamp":"2026-09-16T10:10:27.607Z","parentId":"2c24b952-d94b-421f-a956-c064b5a90dad"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"2dc379c5-451f-4385-a055-2511a0a40c48","content":"Added support for new customers in the ticket form.\n\nThe form now supports:\n\n- Selecting an existing customer by name\n- Adding a new customer inline\n- Entering:\n  - Full name\n  - Email\n  - Department\n- Automatically attaching the new ticket to the newly created customer\n\nThe backend now includes:\n\n```http\nPOST /api/customers\n```\n\nValidation passed:\n\n- Frontend build succeeded\n- Backend tests: `9 passed`\n- New customer API verified\n- Temporary test data removed\n- Original 11 demo customers restored","toolRequests":[]},"id":"9cb3459e-8931-4970-9cbb-6eb1b89b7aec","timestamp":"2026-09-16T10:28:46.632Z","parentId":"9e1b60a7-c7d6-4db6-bd49-32192aa50b1b"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"4a509279-baa5-4da3-a7eb-a7e638fa3ded","content":"I’ll add a shared ticket-edit workflow to the detail page so the current support-team UI and customer-facing UI can both update ticket content. Since authentication is not implemented yet, access is currently shared through the same app; I’ll keep the backend mutation validation in place and expose editing for title, description, priority, and status, with assignment remaining a support-team control.","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_qll2FY4SNMR9HlNPFIVHrxYD","name":"apply_patch","arguments":"{\"input\":\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/frontend/src/App.jsx\\n@@\\n   Download,\\n   Inbox,\\n   LayoutDashboard,\\n+  Pencil,\\n@@\\n   const activity = useApi(`/tickets/${ticketId}/activities`);\\n   const agents = useApi(\\\"/agents\\\");\\n   const [updating, setUpdating] = useState(false);\\n   const [assigning, setAssigning] = useState(false);\\n+  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);\\n+  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\\n@@\\n   const updateAssignee = async (assignedToId) => {\\n@@\\n     setAssigning(false);\\n   };\\n+  const saveTicket = async (event) => {\\n+    event.preventDefault();\\n+    setSaving(true);\\n+    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);\\n+    const response = await fetch(`${API}/tickets/${ticketId}`, {\\n+      method: \\\"PATCH\\\",\\n+      headers: { \\\"Content-Type\\\": \\\"application/json\\\" },\\n+      body: JSON.stringify({\\n+        title: form.get(\\\"title\\\"),\\n+        description: form.get(\\\"description\\\"),\\n+        priority: form.get(\\\"priority\\\"),\\n+        status: form.get(\\\"status\\\"),\\n+      }),\\n+    });\\n+    if (response.ok) window.location.reload();\\n+    setSaving(false);\\n+  };\\n@@\\n           <div className=\\\"detail-actions\\\">\\n+            <button className=\\\"edit-ticket-button\\\" onClick={() => setEditing(!editing)}>\\n+              <Pencil size={14} /> {editing ? \\\"Close editor\\\" : \\\"Edit ticket\\\"}\\n+            </button>\\n             <span>Update status</span>\\n@@\\n         <div className=\\\"detail-grid\\\">\\n           <article className=\\\"detail-card\\\">\\n+            {editing && (\\n+              <form className=\\\"edit-ticket-form\\\" onSubmit={saveTicket}>\\n+                <h3>Edit ticket</h3>\\n+                <label>\\n+                  Title\\n+                  <input name=\\\"title\\\" defaultValue={item.title} required />\\n+                </label>\\n+                <label>\\n+                  Description\\n+                  <textarea name=\\\"description\\\" defaultValue={item.description} required />\\n+                </label>\\n+                <div className=\\\"edit-fields\\\">\\n+                  <label>\\n+                    Priority\\n+                    <select name=\\\"priority\\\" defaultValue={item.priority}>\\n+                      <option>NORMAL</option>\\n+                      <option>HIGH</option>\\n+                      <option>URGENT</option>\\n+                    </select>\\n+                  </label>\\n+                  <label>\\n+                    Status\\n+                    <select name=\\\"status\\\" defaultValue={item.status}>\\n+                      <option>OPEN</option>\\n+                      <option>IN_PROGRESS</option>\\n+                      <option>WAITING</option>\\n+                      <option>RESOLVED</option>\\n+                    </select>\\n+                  </label>\\n+                </div>\\n+                <button className=\\\"primary-button\\\" disabled={saving} type=\\\"submit\\\">\\n+                  {saving ? \\\"Saving...\\\" : \\\"Save changes\\\"}\\n+                </button>\\n+              </form>\\n+            )}\\n             <div className=\\\"detail-badges\\\">\\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Nakul_Saraf_Auriga_Assessment/frontend/src/styles.css\\n@@\\n .customer-toggle{border:0;background:transparent;color:#4e746a;cursor:pointer;font:600 12px 'DM Sans';padding:0;text-align:left}\\n+.edit-ticket-button{border:1px solid #cfded5;background:#fff;color:#3d6854;border-radius:6px;padding:8px 10px;font:600 11px 'DM Sans';cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px}.edit-ticket-form{border-bottom:1px solid #edf1ee;padding-bottom:22px;margin-bottom:22px}.edit-ticket-form label{display:block;color:#526b63;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;margin:12px 0}.edit-ticket-form input,.edit-ticket-form textarea,.edit-ticket-form select{display:block;width:100%;margin-top:6px;border:1px solid #dce5df;border-radius:6px;padding:10px;background:#fff;color:#385d54;font:14px 'DM Sans'}.edit-ticket-form textarea{min-height:105px;resize:vertical}.edit-fields{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px}.edit-ticket-form .primary-button{margin-top:16px}\\n*** End Patch\",\"explanation\":\"Add a shared ticket editor for title, description, priority, and status, while retaining support assignment controls.\"}","type":"function"}],"reasoningText":""},"id":"9a46121b-8883-4a3b-b3b7-ecd13eac6447","timestamp":"2026-09-16T10:33:18.915Z","parentId":"f38579f5-a340-4506-91a8-fc746ee11c35"}
